
===== 2026-10-02T10:11:28 | full_benchmark.py --help =====
usage: full_benchmark.py [-h] [--corpus CORPUS] [--reader-wps READER_WPS]
                         [--models-dir MODELS_DIR] [--state-file STATE_FILE]
                         [--results-file RESULTS_FILE] [--rung RUNG]
                         [--roster ROSTER] [--dry-run] [--force]
                         [--kv-quant-k {q8_0,q4_0,q4_1,q5_0,q5_1,iq4_nl}]
                         [--kv-quant-v {q8_0,q4_0,q4_1,q5_0,q5_1,iq4_nl}]
                         [--thinking] [--no-git] [--min-rung MIN_RUNG]
                         [--max-rung MAX_RUNG] [--no-thinking]
                         families [families ...]

end-to-end benchmark: acquire each family's Q8_0 rung and run the protocol v4
ladder - the depth-scored ladder table is the ranking

positional arguments:
  families              family specs: "model_repo" or "model_repo=source_repo"

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --corpus CORPUS
  --reader-wps READER_WPS
                        the k=1 guarantee line in WORDS per second: worst turn
                        at the reference depth must never fall below this
                        (default 5.0 w/s = 300 wpm, Brysbaert 2019 - match the
                        fast reader)
  --models-dir MODELS_DIR
  --state-file STATE_FILE
  --results-file RESULTS_FILE
  --rung RUNG           the quant to benchmark (default Q8_0; e.g. Q4_K_M -
                        session 34's context-over-parameters test)
  --roster ROSTER       restrict the run's notes to these families (comma-
                        separated, as named in the family specs); the state
                        file accumulates across studies (addendum 41/56)
  --dry-run             READ-ONLY pre-flight (addendum 79): lists every repo,
                        verifies tooling, runs the RAM/disk feasibility checks
                        and reports each family's acquisition plan WITHOUT
                        downloading, converting, benching or touching the
                        state file. THE WAY OF WORKING: always run the command
                        with --dry-run first, read the pre-flight report, then
                        issue it without.
  --force               redo families that already have a selection
  --kv-quant-k {q8_0,q4_0,q4_1,q5_0,q5_1,iq4_nl}
                        session 35, addendum 7: quantize ONLY the K cache to
                        this type (V keeps its own setting; both None =
                        default f16).
  --kv-quant-v {q8_0,q4_0,q4_1,q5_0,q5_1,iq4_nl}
                        session 35, addendum 7: quantize ONLY the V cache to
                        this type (K keeps its own setting; both None =
                        default f16).
  --thinking            thinking-model category: benchmark with thinking
                        enabled (same worst-turn gate; reasoning measured
                        descriptively). Use separate --state-file/--results-
                        file for this category.
  --no-git              skip the git tail (session 34 addendum 15: the commit-
                        and-push of the run's artifacts is now DEFAULT - the
                        git interface is layer 3, like the hub and llama.cpp;
                        the old --git-commit opt-in is superseded)
  --min-rung MIN_RUNG   session 34 (addendum 9/10): the ladder's first rung
                        (default: the protocol v4 base 8192); stored in the
                        state as ladder_min_rung so every subsequent run of
                        the family climbs the same grid
  --max-rung MAX_RUNG   session 35 (addendum 9): the gallop's hard stop - the
                        ladder never climbs past this rung. With --min-rung N
                        --max-rung N the run is a single-rung probe: speed +
                        fwe at exactly N, pass or fail, no search; stored in
                        the state as ladder_max_rung
  --no-thinking         hybrid models, non-thinking category: run with
                        thinking disabled (chat-template kwargs
                        enable_thinking=false; first-turn dump check confirms
                        no reasoning appears)
